######## PR #73 (2026-10-22T04:24:03Z) M3 B1: native profile resolver (aitools-profile.sh/.ps1)
https://github.com/nobul-tech/aitools/pull/73  +2481/-0 files=5
FILES: .aitools/channel/handoffs/2026-10-21_10cf255a-c-missions/S3-Profile/S3-Profile-Review-pr73.md, .github/workflows/check.yml, scripts/aitools-profile.ps1, scripts/aitools-profile.sh, tests/profile/test-profile.sh

## Summary

M3 batch 1: one native resolver for "which dotprofile" and "which machine profile is this machine", as two implementations with identical behaviour: `scripts/aitools-profile.sh` (macOS/Linux) and `scripts/aitools-profile.ps1` (Windows). It has no node dependency. Commands: `user-repo-path`, `machine-id`, `hostname`, `resolve`, `derive-config`, `snapshot`, `register`. No caller uses it yet; the next batches wire it in.

## Why

- **P-Q3**: native bash plus PowerShell, not `aitools-profile.js`.
- **P-Q2**: raw machine ids (macOS IOPlatformUUID, Windows MachineGuid, Linux /etc/machine-id).
- **P-Q5**: on macOS the hostname comes from exactly one command, `scutil --get LocalHostName`. It reports `nobul-mac` here, while the kernel `hostname` reports `Mac`, which comes from DNS/DHCP and is never used.
- **Resolution order:** follows the approved `reference/user-repo.md` draft (R18), "Resolution" section:
  - Dotprofile: env > `--user-repo-path` > config > convention > scan > none.
  - Machine: override > claude-code-web environment > machine.id > config alias > hostname (warns) > none.
  - On none it reports an error and never uses another profile's values.

**JSON on the bash side.** Bash cannot parse JSON, and perl is the harness's string tool. JSON::PP would reorder keys, and it is missing from minimal Linux perl-base images. So the script embeds a small, module-free perl engine that keeps key order and writes the node `JSON.stringify(v, null, 2)` layout. PS7 `ConvertTo-Json` produces the same bytes, checked byte for byte on the live dotprofile `profile.json`. This matters because `profile.json` is shared through git and written from three runtimes.

## Changes

- `scripts/aitools-profile.sh` (new): the resolver plus the embedded perl JSON engine. It sources aitools-lib, writes only detail lines to deploy.log, and puts data on stdout (the console `log` is overridden to stderr).
- `scripts/aitools-profile.ps1` (new): the PowerShell implementation with the same commands, options, output and exit codes. It uses `-DateKind String` when available (PS 7.5+), so ISO dates in profile.json stay strings. Its Windows OS guard can be bypassed only for tests (`AITOOLS_PROFILE_TEST_ANY_OS=1`).
- `tests/profile/test-profile.sh` (new): 180 assertions. Every case runs through both implementations and compares values, exit codes and byte-identical output, including the files `register` writes.
- `.github/workflows/check.yml`: adds the tests on macOS (bash 5 and `/bin/bash` 3.2) and Linux (adds the claude-code-web case). The Windows runner runs a native `machine-id`, `hostname` and `resolve` without a dotprofile; this is the first execution of the MachineGuid path.

## Verification

- `bash tests/profile/test-profile.sh`: **180 passed, 0 failed** (bash 5.3.15 + pwsh 7.6.3, macOS).
- `AITOOLS_TEST_BASH=/bin/bash /bin/bash tests/profile/test-profile.sh --bash-only`: **135 passed, 0 failed** (bash 3.2.57).
- Isolated-HOME smoke against a copy of the live `profile.json`: `resolve` gives `alias=nobul-mac via=alias company=nobul-tech` in 0.24 s. `register` added only the `machine.id` line; every other byte is unchanged.
- `bash -n` (3.2) and the pwsh parser: clean.
- `check-pre-commit.sh`: 10 PASS, 4 WARN, 1 FAIL. The failure is step 6, the known pre-existing `harvesting/...build-deploy-prefix.sh` 100644; the new `.sh` files are 100755.
- `check-pre-push.sh`: 6 PASS, 1 SKIP, 3 WARN, 0 FAIL.

## Not run

- **Linux and Windows locally:** there is no container runtime and no Windows machine here. CI covers both on this PR.
- **The commander's workstation:** the Windows MachineGuid path stays UNVERIFIED there until `aitools user init` runs on it (B7).
- **build-deploy** (pre-commit step 3 WARN): nothing in deploy/ embeds the resolver yet, and it must not run against the real HOME.
- **RELEASE_NOTES.md:** not updated (WARN). The release entry belongs with the batch that switches callers over.

## Protected-file drafts awaiting approval

- **Intent headers of both new scripts:** the approved R22 intent for `aitools-profile.js`, adapted by:
  - naming the native pair (`setup-user-config.sh/.ps1` writer note unchanged);
  - splitting "Consumed by" per platform;
  - dropping the JS-only consumer `profile-check.js (require)`.

  The Purpose and NOT clauses are verbatim. Please confirm, or send the wording you want.

## Follow-ups

- **B1b (next PR):** `exception-get` / `exception-set` subcommands (N-Q1 via P-Q3; S4's X-B1). These are the only readers and writers of `toolExceptions`.
- **B2:** the lib wrappers `resolve_user_repo_path` / `Resolve-UserRepoPath` in aitools-lib. They wait for SOURCE-TRUTH (it owns aitools-lib.*).
- **B6/B7:** `check_profile` and `user init` switch to the resolver, and profile-check.js loses its `os.hostname()` (DNS) matching.
- **Known edge cases:**
  - PowerShell property names are case-insensitive, so aliases that differ only by case collide.
  - Non-integer numbers are normalized through perl's `%.15g`; node prints the shortest round-trip form. profile.json holds only the integer `version` today.

######## PR #76 (2026-10-22T04:37:58Z) M3 B1b: tool exception store (exception-get/set) in the native resolver
https://github.com/nobul-tech/aitools/pull/76  +626/-58 files=3
FILES: scripts/aitools-profile.ps1, scripts/aitools-profile.sh, tests/profile/test-profile.sh

## Summary

M3 batch 1b (S4's X-B1): adds the tool-exception store's reader and writer as `exception-get` / `exception-set` subcommands of the native resolver, `scripts/aitools-profile.sh` / `.ps1`.

**Stacked on #73.** This branch starts from `feature/m3-b1-profile-resolver`, so the diff shows #73's commit until #73 merges. This PR's own commit is the last one.

## Why

- **N-Q1:** sets the toolExceptions shape. The Session Commander's 2026-10-21 clarification moves the engine from `aitools-profile.js` to the native pair (P-Q3).
- **N-Q2:** dotprofile writes auto-commit and push.
- **Decision tree rev 5, section 8:**
  - The writer is allowed when `via` is id, environment, alias or override.
  - It is refused when `via` is hostname or none, or when there is no dotprofile.
  - It never writes in a dry run.

## Changes

- **`exception-get --slug S`:** resolves this machine's profile and prints `found` plus the stored fields. Matching the entry against the detected install stays with the caller, because it needs the registry's `versionedPath`.
- **`exception-set` writes the entry:**
  - Arguments: `--slug S --platform P --method M --path PATH --version V --by SCRIPT [--reason R] [--decided-at UTC]`.
  - It is the only writer of `toolExceptions`.
  - The entry follows the `reference/user-repo.md` shape and key order.
  - The write path is temp → validate → backup → move.
- **`exception-set` commit and push:**
  - It commits **only** `profile.json`; other staged or modified dotprofile files are left alone. Today the live dotprofile has 23 unrelated modified files.
  - It pushes with `GIT_TERMINAL_PROMPT=0`, so a push never hangs on a credential prompt.
  - On a machine without a git identity it commits as the profile's `identity.git`.
  - A commit or push failure is a warning; the file is already written.
- **`exception-set` exits:**
  - Exit 3 (refused): no dotprofile profile.json; machine profile matched by hostname only or not at all; or the profile came from `AITOOLS_PROFILE_FILE`, the read-only snapshot in generated deploy scripts.
  - `AITOOLS_DRY_RUN=1` reports `dry-run` and writes nothing.
  - An identical entry reports `unchanged` and writes nothing.
- **Shared helper:** `resolve` and the new commands share one profile-locating helper (`locate_profile` / `Find-ProfileFile`). `resolve`'s output is unchanged; all B1 tests pass.
- **Tests:** 16 exception cases. Each runs against a fresh fixture per implementation, with a byte comparison of the written profile.json and a real push to a bare remote.

## Verification

- `bash tests/profile/test-profile.sh`: **251 passed, 0 failed** (bash 5.3.15 + pwsh 7.6.3).
- `AITOOLS_TEST_BASH=/bin/bash /bin/bash tests/profile/test-profile.sh --bash-only`: **185 passed, 0 failed** (bash 3.2.57).
- A manual PowerShell run against a scratch git dotprofile wrote the entry and the backup. Without a git identity it reported the commit failure as a warning, which led to the identity fallback; that fallback is now tested.
- `bash -n` (3.2) and the pwsh parser: clean. `check-pre-commit`: only the known step 6 FAIL.

- **Linux and Windows locally:** CI covers both.
- **No caller yet:** the `tool_exception_get` / `tool_exception_record` wrappers belong in aitools-lib (M2 B2/B3), which SOURCE-TRUTH owns until its PR merges.

  - the consumer list names the planned tool exception wrappers.

## Changes

### 9462ed0 (Step 5 -> writer)
- **`aitools-install.sh` Step 5:** the inline node merge becomes one call. It passes `--repos-path`, `--repo-path`, `--drives-json` and `--dry-run`.
- **`aitools-install.ps1` Step 5:** the same call, made in-process.

### 69b6420 (Review3 fixes + logging audit)
- **m1, the writer's log lines are missing from `deploy.jsonl`.**
  - Step 5 now captures the writer's console lines (bash: stdout; PowerShell: `6>&1`, still in-process).
  - It prints them and mirrors each `[ts] [script] [level] msg` line to `deploy.jsonl`, keeping the writer's own ts, script and level.
  - Shared helper: `jsonl_write` / `Write-JsonlRecord`. Bash now JSON-escapes the message: before this, a quote in a message produced an invalid JSONL line. PowerShell now uses the same key order as bash.
- **m2, the writer's warnings are not counted.** The relay counts the writer's `warn` lines in the installer's WARNINGS. Errors stay as they were: a failing writer exits 1 and counts as one installer error.
- **m3, a relative repos path is rejected by the writer.**
  - Step 3 now makes the path absolute, relative to the current directory. That is where the old installer's `mkdir -p` created it.
  - The conversion is logged. The writer's guard against relative paths stays.
- **m4, the validator comment.** It now says `validate_json_config` / `ValidateJsonConfig` stay in the installers (B2 kept them out of the lib). In bash the comment sits directly above the function.
- **m5, the dry run writes a file.** The dry run now merges into a system temp file, and nothing under `~/.aitools` is created or changed.
- **Audit fixes in the writer:**
  - The `mktemp -d` result is checked.
  - Creating `~/.aitools` is checked.
  - The double-slash check is now one perl call with its exit code checked. Before, a two-stage pipe could die of SIGPIPE under pipefail, which made `set -e` exit silently.
- **Tests (`tests/profile/test-config.sh`):**
  - C8b: a dry run with `~/.aitools` read-only. It fails on the old writer.
  - Installer-helper cases for both shells: each installer's helper block is extracted and run against the real writer.

## Interface: setup-user-config.sh / .ps1

| Item | Contract |
|---|---|
| Arguments (bash / PowerShell) | `--repos-path`/`-ReposPath`, `--repo-path`/`-RepoPath` (absolute paths only), `--drives-json`/`-DrivesJson` (JSON array), `--github-user`/`-GithubUser`, `--user-repo-path`/`-UserRepoPath` (absolute), `--machine-alias`/`-MachineAlias`, `--dry-run`/`-DryRun`, `--force`/`-Force` (accepted, no effect), `--help`/`-Help`. With no arguments it re-derives the values from profile.json and keeps the other keys. |
| Env vars | `HOME`; `AITOOLS_DRY_RUN=1` (same as dry run); `AITOOLS_LOG_DIR`; `AITOOLS_SUMMARY_FILE`; `AITOOLS_ENVIRONMENT` (lib). Through the resolver: `AITOOLS_USER_REPO_PATH` (used for this run only, never saved), `AITOOLS_MACHINE_ALIAS`, `AITOOLS_PROFILE_FILE`. Tests only: `AITOOLS_PROFILE_TEST_MACHINE_ID`, `AITOOLS_PROFILE_TEST_HOSTNAME`, `AITOOLS_PROFILE_TEST_ANY_OS`. |
| Files read | `~/.aitools/config.json`; the dotprofile `profile.json`, through `aitools-profile.sh/.ps1 derive-config`; the scripts next to it: `aitools-profile.*`, plus `aitools-json.pl` (bash) or `aitools-json.ps1` (PowerShell). |
| Files written | `~/.aitools/config.json`, written as temp file beside it -> validate -> `config.json.bak.<ts>` backup -> move. A dry run writes nothing under `~/.aitools`. |
| Summary rows (`aitools config`) | **OK:** `created`, `updated`, `verified`, `dry-run: would update`. **WARN:** `rebuilt (was invalid)`; an OK row becomes WARN when the run warned. **ERROR:** `bad arguments`, `perl not found` (bash only), `resolver missing`, `temp dir failed` (new), `write failed`, `derivation failed`, `merge failed`, `validation failed`, `missing bootstrap values`. **DETAIL:** `<key> updated`. |
| Other rows | ERROR `profile.json` `invalid (see log)`; ERROR `machine profile` `no machine match`; ACTION rows. |
| Log sinks | `deploy.log`, and console lines on stdout (bash) or `Write-Host` (PowerShell). Not `deploy.jsonl`: a caller that wants JSONL relays the lines, as the installers now do. |
| Exit codes | `0`: done, possibly with warnings. `1`: any error. `--help`: 0. Never 2. |
| Interaction | Never prompts; safe for a script to call (deploy-harness). |
| Changes vs cc597ec | The dry-run temp file location. `temp dir failed` is a new ERROR row. `write failed` now also covers "could not create `~/.aitools`". Everything else is unchanged. |

######## PR #80 (2026-10-23T00:37:47Z) M3 B2: aitools-lib profile and tool-exception wrappers
https://github.com/nobul-tech/aitools/pull/80  +1745/-346 files=18
FILES: .aitools/channel/handoffs/2026-10-21_10cf255a-c-missions/S3-M3-LibFix/S3-M3-LibFix-Review-pr80.md, .aitools/channel/handoffs/2026-10-21_10cf255a-c-missions/S3-M3-LibFix/S3-M3-LibFix-report.md, .aitools/channel/handoffs/2026-10-21_10cf255a-c-missions/S3-M3-LibFix/S3-M3-LibFix-tree-draft.md, .aitools/channel/handoffs/2026-10-21_10cf255a-c-missions/S3-Profile/S3-Profile-Review2-pr76-77.md, .claude/rules/config-file-safety.md, .cursor/rules/config-file-safety.mdc, .github/workflows/check.yml, scripts/aitools-install.ps1, scripts/aitools-install.sh, scripts/aitools-lib.ps1, scripts/aitools-lib.sh, scripts/aitools-profile.ps1, scripts/aitools-profile.sh, scripts/setup-user-config.ps1, scripts/setup-user-config.sh, tests/profile/test-config.sh, tests/profile/test-lib.sh, tests/profile/test-profile.sh

## Summary

M3 batch 2: the aitools-lib wrappers every consumer will use for "which dotprofile", "which machine profile" and the tool-exception store:

| bash | PowerShell |
|------|------------|
| `resolve_user_repo_path` | `Resolve-UserRepoPath` |
| `resolve_machine_profile` | `Resolve-MachineProfile` |
| `tool_exception_get` | `Get-ToolException` |
| `tool_exception_record` | `Set-ToolException` |

No caller is switched yet. **Stacked on #79** (and through it #77 and #76); this PR's own commits are the last three (18218ce, dae07fe, 28e0f5b).

## Contract

Result names are final as of dae07fe; 28e0f5b only fixed what `AITLIB_TOOL_EXC_RESULT` holds on failure. Every result carries one lib prefix: bash `AITLIB_*`, PowerShell `$script:AitLib*`. Private helpers are `_aitlib_*` (bash) and `*-AitLib*` (PowerShell). The lib makes no top-level assignment in this section. A dot-sourced PowerShell lib shares the caller's case-insensitive script scope, so a top-level assignment would overwrite a caller's parameter (Review3 B1).

| Function | bash results | PowerShell results | Return |
|---|---|---|---|
| `resolve_user_repo_path` / `Resolve-UserRepoPath` | `AITLIB_USER_REPO_PATH`, `AITLIB_USER_REPO_VIA` | `$script:AitLibUserRepoPath`, `$script:AitLibUserRepoVia` | 0 found, 1 none (shared-only), 3 error |
| `resolve_machine_profile` / `Resolve-MachineProfile` | `AITLIB_MACHINE_ALIAS`, `AITLIB_MACHINE_VIA`, `AITLIB_PROFILE_FILE`, `AITLIB_PROFILE_NAME`, `AITLIB_PROFILE_COMPANY`, `AITLIB_IDENTITY_GIT_NAME`, `AITLIB_IDENTITY_GIT_EMAIL`, `AITLIB_IDENTITY_GITHUB` | `$script:AitLibMachineAlias`, `AitLibMachineVia`, `AitLibProfileFile`, `AitLibProfileName`, `AitLibProfileCompany`, `AitLibIdentityGitName`, `AitLibIdentityGitEmail`, `AitLibIdentityGithub` | 0 resolved, 1 no dotprofile, 3 no match / error |
| `tool_exception_get` / `Get-ToolException` | `AITLIB_TOOL_EXC_FOUND` (true/false), `AITLIB_TOOL_EXC_PLATFORM`, `AITLIB_TOOL_EXC_METHOD`, `AITLIB_TOOL_EXC_PATH`, `AITLIB_TOOL_EXC_DECIDED_AT` | return value `@{ Found; Platform; Method; Path; DecidedAt }`, or `$null` when unavailable (no result variable) | bash 0 looked up, 1 unavailable |
| `tool_exception_record` / `Set-ToolException` | `AITLIB_TOOL_EXC_RESULT` (created/updated/unchanged/dry-run/refused/failed; never empty after the call), `AITLIB_TOOL_EXC_PUSHED` (true/false) | `$script:AitLibToolExcResult`, `$script:AitLibToolExcPushed` | 0 written or nothing to write, 1 failed, 3 refused |

`TOOL_EXC_*` moved to `AITLIB_TOOL_EXC_*`, so the rule has no exception.

**How callers copy them.** Read a result only after the call. Under `set -u`, a bash result that was never set is an unbound-variable error. Copy it into your own variable right after the call:

```bash
rc=0; resolve_user_repo_path || rc=$?
USER_REPO_PATH="$AITLIB_USER_REPO_PATH"
```

```powershell
$rc = Resolve-UserRepoPath
$userRepoPath = $script:AitLibUserRepoPath
```

**Summary rows.**
- The wrapper writes the one ACTION row for its own failure:
  - "Run: aitools user init (pick or register this machine's profile)";
  - "run aitools user init to remember tool choices" (refused);
  - "see deploy.log -- a tool choice (tool exception) was not saved" (failed: resolver missing, usage error, crash);
  - "run git push in <dotprofile>".
- It writes that row once per run: `_aitlib_action_once` / `Write-AitLibActionOnce` skip a row the run summary already holds.
- Callers do not add their own ACTION for the same failure. A caller that needs different text records a "Contract change" section in its PR.
- ERROR rows ("dotprofile", "machine profile") are written on every failing call.

## Why

- **P-Q3:** native resolver.
- **Tree rev 5 section 7:** helpers return results in globals, never in bash stdout; no helper returns 2.
- **Tree rev 5 section 8:** the reader and writer contract.
- **D-TS12:** a successful step stays OK.
- **N-Q2:** dotprofile writes commit and push.
- **Review3 (#80) B1, M1, m1-m4 and commander decision 3 (logging audit):** fixed in dae07fe; see Changes.

## Changes

- **`scripts/aitools-lib.sh`:** the four wrappers plus private helpers.
  - They run `aitools-profile.sh` under the caller's bash.
  - Under Git Bash on Windows, where the .sh refuses to run, they use `aitools-profile.ps1` through pwsh and strip CR.
  - `AITOOLS_PROFILE_SH` / `AITOOLS_PROFILE_PS1` point them at the embedded copy in generated deploy scripts.
- **`scripts/aitools-lib.ps1`:** the four wrappers, calling the resolver in-process. Nothing reaches the output stream except each function's declared return value.
- **Return codes:**
  - resolvers: 0 found, 1 shared-only, 3 error;
  - `tool_exception_get`: 0, or 1 when the store is unavailable;
  - `tool_exception_record`: 0 written, 1 failed, 3 refused.
- **Logging:**
  - Resolver warnings become detail lines, because setup-user-config reports them once per run.
  - No machine match: ERROR `machine profile` + ACTION.
  - A failed commit or push of an exception: warning + ACTION `run git push in <dotprofile>`.
- **Tests and CI:** `tests/profile/test-lib.sh` (new) runs the cases through both libs and checks the return code, globals, counters and summary rows. CI runs it on macOS (bash 5 and 3.2) and Linux.
- **28e0f5b (independent review of dae07fe):**
  - **M1:** an exit 0 or 1 is trusted only with the resolver's `via` line. Otherwise it becomes rc 2 with an `error` line, in both shells.
  - The pwsh `-Command` wrapper catches throws and parse failures (exit 2) and passes `-ExecutionPolicy Bypass`.
  - The `cygpath -w` results are checked.
  - PowerShell captures the resolver's `[Console]::Error` into deploy.log.
  - The record result is never empty, and a failure points to deploy.log.
  - Tests: eget-found, and broken resolvers (silent exit 0 and 1, throw, parse error).
- **dae07fe (Review3 fixes):**
  - **B1:** no top-level assignment in the PowerShell section. `$script:AitoolsLibDir` was removed; `$PSScriptRoot` inside the function names the lib's directory.
  - **M1:** the prefixes in the Contract section.
  - **m1:** `result=unchanged` and `result=dry-run` no longer warn "saved but not pushed" and get no push ACTION.
  - **m3:** one ACTION per run.
  - **m2, Git Bash branch:**
    - `--path` and the resolver path go to pwsh in Windows form (`cygpath -w`);
    - pwsh output is forced to UTF-8;
    - CR is stripped from stderr too;
    - `AITLIB_PROFILE_VIA_PWSH=1` forces the branch on any OS, for tests;
    - on Windows itself the branch is still UNVERIFIED (code comment).
  - **m4:** a `mktemp` failure is checked and logged as a warning.
  - **Logging audit:**
    - `cygpath -u` failure falls back to the input;
    - an empty `profileFile` no longer gives "run git push in .";
    - no unchecked suppression remains in the section.
  - **Tests:** 72 checks (was 40). New cases:
    - caller variables and parameters survive sourcing the lib and calling the wrappers, in both shells (the B1 regression: a `[string]$UserRepoPath` param);

######## PR #81 (2026-10-23T01:29:20Z) Make the managed-file review diff readable (local -> repo, labelled, coloured)
https://github.com/nobul-tech/aitools/pull/81  +590/-94 files=8
FILES: .aitools/channel/handoffs/2026-10-21_10cf255a-c-missions/S3-DiffUX/S3-DiffUX-Review-verdict.md, .aitools/channel/handoffs/2026-10-21_10cf255a-c-missions/S3-DiffUX/S3-DiffUX-handback.md, reference/managed-file-deployment.md, scripts/aitools-lib.ps1, scripts/aitools-lib.sh, shared/skills/delegate/SKILL.md, tests/managed-files/test-managed-files.ps1, tests/managed-files/test-managed-files.sh

## Summary

The managed-file review diff now reads as "what [o]verwrite changes on this machine": LOCAL -> SOURCE, with both paths in the headers, a one-line legend, red/green colour on a terminal, and menu lines that say what each choice does to the lines shown. Bash and PS1 print byte-identical output.

What the commander sees now:

```
[REVIEW] /Users/you/.claude/rules/foo.md differs from source.

  red - = only on this machine   green + = only in the repo

--- this machine  /Users/you/.claude/rules/foo.md
+++ repo          /Users/you/repos/aitools-you/claude/rules/foo.md
@@ -1,3 +1,3 @@
 # Title
-my local line
+repo line
 end

  [o]verwrite       : this machine gets the green + lines (backup kept)
  [a]ccept & adopt  : the repo gets the red - lines (your version) -> /Users/you/repos/aitools-you/claude/rules/foo.md
  [m]erge & adopt   : AI combines both; written to this machine and /Users/you/repos/aitools-you/claude/rules/foo.md
  [s]kip            : nothing written; asked again next run
  [x]abort          : stop this script (exit 2)
  choice [o/a/m/s/x]:
```

## Why

Commander report, 2026-10-21 (mission DIFF-UX):
1. the diff ran source -> local, the reverse of the usual reading;
2. lines had no side marker, colour or legend;
3. the menu did not say what each choice does to the lines shown;
4. PS1 printed a different format (a `Compare-Object` listing).

## Changes

- `scripts/aitools-lib.sh`: new `_review_diff` and `_review_colour`. `prompt_diff_review` prints the legend, the coloured diff and the new menu text.
- `scripts/aitools-lib.ps1`: new `Get-ReviewDiff` and `Test-ReviewColour`. `Prompt-DiffReview` does the same as bash. The `Compare-Object` display is gone.
  - Both libraries run the same `git diff --no-index --no-color --no-ext-diff --no-textconv --diff-algorithm=myers --indent-heuristic -U3` on temp files, so the hunks match on both.
  - git's own headers are replaced by `--- this machine  <LOCAL>` and `+++ repo          <adopt path | source (embedded)>`.
  - PS1 reads git's output as UTF-8 straight from the process stream (encoding rule).
- Colour is on unless `NO_COLOR` is set or `TERM=dumb`. The diff appears only on an interactive terminal. `deploy.log` stays plain text and keeps the full diff as `[detail]` lines at every size.
- Truncation works the same way on both: when the diff is over 40 lines, the first 30 are shown. PS1 now uses the bash wording.
- If git fails: a WARN is logged, the terminal shows `(diff unavailable -- see deploy log)`, and the menu still works.
- No change to: the choices, their order, their return values, non-interactive/EOF/`--force` behaviour, or the skip and abort text.
- **The AI merge input is left as it was** (bash still gets the old source -> local `diff -u`, PS1 the `Compare-Object` listing). Only the display changed. See Follow-ups.
- `reference/managed-file-deployment.md`: "Menus" and "Diff Mechanism Detail" now describe the new display (approved in the mission launch).
- `tests/managed-files/test-managed-files.sh` / `.ps1`: new output assertions. They check direction, header paths and padding, legend placement, all five menu lines, colour on, colour off with `NO_COLOR` and with `TERM=dumb`, no diff or colour when non-interactive, a plain-text log, >40-line truncation with the full diff in the log, and git failure. They run under an isolated HOME with the existing pty harness. `run_lib`/`Run-Lib` now set `TERM` (default `xterm`) and clear `NO_COLOR`, so colour tests are deterministic.

## Review fixes (commit 126083e)

The independent reviewer (S3-DiffUX-Review) found three problems; all are fixed. Its verdict and the resolution are posted as a PR comment.

- **B1, blocking:** a diff over about 64 KB aborted the setup run. The cause was `printf | head` under pipefail (SIGPIPE). Truncation now uses perl, which reads all of its input. A test covers a 330 KB diff.
- **N1:** the git diff options are pinned against user config. `diff.suppressBlankEmpty` and `diff.interHunkContext` are set with `-c`, and `GIT_DIFF_OPTS` is removed from git's environment.
- **N2:** `--text` added, so content with a NUL byte still gets a text diff.

## Commander follow-ups (commit 7bdaec0)

- The redundant context lines (`source = ...`, `local = ...`, `repo = ... (adopt target)`) are gone in both libraries. The diff headers now name both files. Tests assert the lines are absent, and the reference doc shows the `[REVIEW]` line above the legend.
- `shared/skills/delegate/SKILL.md`, step 5: the independent reviewer posts its verdict with `gh pr comment`. GitHub refuses `--request-changes` on a PR opened by the same account; this was seen on this PR.

## Verification (macOS, Darwin arm64)

- `bash tests/managed-files/test-managed-files.sh`: 57 passed, 0 failed. With `/bin/bash` 3.2.57 first on PATH: 57/57.
- `pwsh -NoProfile -File tests/managed-files/test-managed-files.ps1`: 39 passed, 0 failed.
- Mutation check: the new tests against the old libraries fail 11 cases on each side, as they should.
- Parity: bash and PS1 output compared on a pty for a multi-hunk sample with blank lines and UTF-8 (`café →`). From the legend to the prompt, the plain text is byte-identical.
- `/bin/bash -n scripts/aitools-lib.sh` and the pwsh `Parser::ParseFile` on `scripts/aitools-lib.ps1`: clean.
- `check-post-push` step 29 menu-parity regex: the `[letter]` sets are unchanged on both sides (same `printf`/`Console.WriteLine` lines).
- `check-pre-commit.sh`: two FAILs, neither from this change:
  - step 6: known, harvesting file mode 100644;
  - step 20: dead `reference/tool-support-decision-tree.md` and `scripts/gen-tool-specs.py` references already on main.

## Not run

- Windows: Git Bash `display_path`, PS1 colour in Windows Terminal/conhost, `git` resolved through `ProcessStartInfo` on Windows.

- The real `aitools` entry point.

## Protected-file drafts awaiting approval

None. The only protected file changed is `reference/managed-file-deployment.md`, and the mission launch approved that edit.

## Follow-ups

- **AI merge diff (suggest: a separate small PR).** The merge prompt (`_ai_prompt_merge`) labels its diff "unified diff: local vs source" and says "apply ONLY the changes shown in the diff" to LOCAL. In bash it actually gets source -> local, and in PS1 a `Compare-Object` listing. Feeding it the new LOCAL -> SOURCE `REVIEW_DIFF` would match its own wording and make bash and PS1 agree. That is a prompt change, so agentic-standards prompt evaluation applies. It was left out here to keep merge behaviour unchanged.
- RELEASE_NOTES entry: none of the recent PRs (#68 onward) added one. Suggest the session commander rolls them into one release entry.

######## PR #83 (2026-10-23T01:13:31Z) M3 B4: setup-user-claude uses the one resolver (no first-profile fallback, literal-safe tokens)
https://github.com/nobul-tech/aitools/pull/83  +425/-183 files=4
FILES: .aitools/channel/handoffs/2026-10-21_10cf255a-c-missions/S3-M3-Claude-Review/S3-M3-Claude-Review-pr83.md, .aitools/channel/handoffs/2026-10-21_10cf255a-c-missions/S3-M3-Claude/S3-M3-Claude-report.md, scripts/setup-user-claude.ps1, scripts/setup-user-claude.sh

## Summary

M3 batch 4: `setup-user-claude.sh` / `.ps1` take the dotprofile path and this machine's profile only from the PR #80 wrappers (`resolve_user_repo_path` / `resolve_machine_profile`, `Resolve-UserRepoPath` / `Resolve-MachineProfile`), on the fixed result names (`AITLIB_*`, `$script:AitLib*`). They read no config.json or profile.json themselves: no alias lookup, no hostname or first-profile fallback (re-key design batch 6, alias-independent part). Interpolation and the adopt filter are literal-safe and single-pass. Logging audited (commander decision 3).

**Base:** the branch merges PR #80's fixed head (28e0f5b) and origin/main (#78, #79). The lib files are identical to #80 (`git diff origin/feature/m3-b2-lib-wrappers -- scripts/aitools-lib.*` is empty); this PR's own changes are the two scripts. Merge #80 first.

## Why

- Review3 #80 M1: callers used names that collided with the lib; they now copy the prefixed results right after each call.
- Re-key design batch 6 (S3-M3-Register keydesign (h)): profiles will be keyed by machine id, so the callers must not read `profiles[alias]`.
- Approved R18 "Resolution" / "Template Resolution"; P-Q5 (macOS hostname = scutil LocalHostName); P-Q13 (`{{PROFILE_NAME}}` wins the same-text tie).
- Incident drafts #109 (`eval` of profile values) and #110 (`&` / `$` in values); S2-Host "first profile" fallback.
- Commander decision 3: logging audit.
- Independent review S3-M3-Claude-Review (https://github.com/nobul-tech/aitools/pull/83#pullrequestreview-5621269178): M1, M2, m1, m4, n2, n5 fixed in a87ffce.

## Changes

- **Resolution:** one call per wrapper; results copied into the script's own variables. No machine match: identity-level values only (the wrapper writes ERROR "machine profile" + one ACTION). No dotprofile (rc 1): shared template, tokens stay visible, WARN. **Dotprofile error (rc 3): nothing is deployed**, ERROR "claude.md" "not deployed: dotprofile unresolved", exit 1 (before, the shared template replaced the personal CLAUDE.md).
- **Interpolation:** one regex pass; values come from the environment (bash/perl) or a MatchEvaluator (PowerShell), so `&`, `$`, `\1`, `@`, `/` and token text inside a value are literal. New token `{{USER_REPO_PATH}}` (`~/...` / `%USERPROFILE%\...`).
- **Adopt filter:** one pass over all values, longest first; equal text keeps the first token (USER_REPO_PATH, PROFILE_NAME, PROFILE_COMPANY, IDENTITY_GIT_NAME, IDENTITY_GIT_EMAIL), so P-Q13 holds. Fixes the live-data corruption `jose@{{PROFILE_COMPANY}}` (company `nobul.tech` inside email `jose@nobul.tech`).
- **Footer:** `- Machine: <os> <arch> (<host>; profile <alias|unresolved>)`. Host from the resolver's source and form (macOS scutil LocalHostName, else `uname -n`; PowerShell COMPUTERNAME; short, lower-case; `unknown` on failure). **Each machine's CLAUDE.md footer changes once, so the next run reviews it once.**
- **Logging:** every exit path writes a summary row (no template, unreadable, empty, interpolation failed, `~/.claude` or rules dir not creatable); bash template read, perl and mkdir are checked; one detail line records both return codes; an unreadable rule source is log_error + tracker `failed`; a failed push of a merged rule adds a WARN row; PowerShell Get-CimInstance failure is caught.
- **CLAUDE.md validation** counts only errors raised during validation (a resolver error no longer reports "claude.md validation failed").
- The `(extracted by build-deploy)` markers are unchanged.

- `bash -n` (bash 5 and /bin/bash 3.2), pwsh parser: clean.

  - **Major M1:** `exception-set` refuses `via=alias` whenever this machine has an id. That case means the profile is unregistered or registered to another machine, and the other machine would later honour the exception. `derive-config` no longer caches a hostname match as `machineAlias`. This is stricter than tree rev 5 section 8; #78 carries the same text for your confirmation.
  - **Minors:**
    - git not on PATH: warning, no crash;
    - an identical re-keep is `unchanged`, with no commit or push;
    - a null entry is handled the same in bash and PowerShell;
    - no misleading second error line;
    - no auto-commit over uncommitted hand edits to profile.json;
    - the identity fallback applies when user.name or user.email is unset;
    - tests for all of these.
- **e6850ad, the #77 fixes:**
  - `//` in path values and relative bootstrap paths are rejected;

######## PR #86 (2026-10-23T00:59:29Z) M3 re-key: machine profiles keyed by machine id
https://github.com/nobul-tech/aitools/pull/86  +6443/-1511 files=26
FILES: .aitools/channel/handoffs/2026-10-21_10cf255a-c-missions/S3-M3-ConfigKey-Review/S3-M3-ConfigKey-Review-pr86-batch4.md, .aitools/channel/handoffs/2026-10-21_10cf255a-c-missions/S3-M3-ConfigKey/S3-M3-ConfigKey-report.md, .aitools/channel/handoffs/2026-10-21_10cf255a-c-missions/S3-M3-JsonRename/S3-M3-JsonRename-report.md, .aitools/channel/handoffs/2026-10-21_10cf255a-c-missions/S3-M3-LibKey-Review/S3-M3-LibKey-Review-pr86-batch3.md, .aitools/channel/handoffs/2026-10-21_10cf255a-c-missions/S3-M3-LibKey/S3-M3-LibKey-report.md, .aitools/channel/handoffs/2026-10-21_10cf255a-c-missions/S3-M3-Resolver/S3-M3-Resolver-Review-pr86-batch2.md, .aitools/channel/handoffs/2026-10-21_10cf255a-c-missions/S3-M3-Resolver/S3-M3-Resolver-report.md, .aitools/channel/handoffs/2026-10-21_10cf255a-c-missions/S3-M3-UserInit-Review/S3-M3-UserInit-Review-pr86-batch5.md, .aitools/channel/handoffs/2026-10-21_10cf255a-c-missions/S3-M3-UserInit/S3-M3-UserInit-report.md, .github/workflows/check.yml, scripts/aitools, scripts/aitools-json.pl, scripts/aitools-json.ps1, scripts/aitools-lib.ps1, scripts/aitools-lib.sh, scripts/aitools-profile.ps1, scripts/aitools-profile.sh, scripts/aitools.ps1, scripts/profile-check.js, scripts/setup-user-config.ps1, scripts/setup-user-config.sh, tests/profile/test-config.sh, tests/profile/test-lib.sh, tests/profile/test-profile.sh, tests/profile/test-user-init.sh, tests/profile/user-init-harness.ps1

## Summary

Stacked on #82 (`feature/m3-b6-user-init`); review only the commits above it. This PR carries batches 1-5 of the re-key design (`S3-M3-Register-keydesign.md`, committed on #82): machine profiles keyed by the raw machine id. **Batch 1 lands now**: an order-preserving key `rename` in both JSON engines. Batches 2-5 (resolver, lib wrappers, config.json, user init) are pushed here by later missions.

## Why

Commander decision 5 (2026-10-22): `profiles.<machineId>` replaces `profiles.<alias>`. The design's `migrate` subcommand (section f) renames every legacy alias key to its lower-cased machine id **in place**, so the profile keeps its position and the dotprofile diff stays one line per machine. Neither engine could rename a key; set + del would move the entry to the end.

## Changes

- `scripts/aitools-json.pl`: new `edit` op `rename<TAB>NEW<TAB>SEG...` (the last SEG is the key to rename). Header documents the contract.
- `scripts/aitools-json.ps1`: new `Rename-JKey ROOT NEW SEG...`, the PowerShell counterpart (keeps order by removing the key and every key after it, then adding them back).
- Same rules and messages in both:
  - `rename: no key given`
  - `rename: '<seg>' not found` (a path segment or the key is absent)
  - `rename: '<seg>' is not an object`
  - `rename: '<NEW>' already exists`: another key equals NEW ignoring case, because PowerShell property names are case-insensitive. Without this, perl could write a file that PowerShell cannot read.
  - NEW equal to the key is a no-op. A case-only rename (`ABC` -> `abc`) is allowed. Path segments match case-sensitively in both engines.
  - perl: exit 2, nothing written. PowerShell: throws (callers catch, as for `Set-JValue`).
- `tests/profile/test-profile.sh`: new section "JSON engines: rename". It has 14 cases (66 assertions) and a small pwsh driver written to the temp dir. It checks position (first, middle, last, top level), that every other byte is kept, rename followed by set, case-only, no-op, all error cases, and that nothing is written on failure. It also checks that perl and pwsh write identical bytes and identical messages.

## Verification (macOS)

- `perl -c`, `bash -n`, pwsh parser: clean.
- Isolated HOME: test-profile.sh 385 passed, 0 failed; test-config.sh 89 passed; test-lib.sh 40 passed. Each ran under `/bin/bash` 3.2 (`AITOOLS_TEST_BASH=/bin/bash`), and test-profile also ran under bash 5.

## Batch 2 -- native resolver keyed by machine id (S3-M3-Resolver)

Commits: b70fc64 (resolver), 3ce5850 (review fixes + `~` / UTF-16 parity), c78bf12 (report + review). Report: `.aitools/channel/handoffs/2026-10-21_10cf255a-c-missions/S3-M3-Resolver/S3-M3-Resolver-report.md`.

### Interface

- **Shape (rev 2.2):** `profiles.<machine id, lower-cased>` (`environment:<name>` on claude-code-web), human name in `alias`, `machine.id` repeats the key. Legacy alias-keyed profiles are still read; `migrate` re-keys them.
- **Output fields** (resolve / derive-config / exception-get / exception-set): `key`, `alias` (field, else key), `via` (override, environment, id, config, alias, hostname, none), `owner` (this, other, unregistered, unknown, absent), `legacy` (true/false). derive-config adds `derived.machineId` (this machine's id-keyed profile only) beside `derived.machineAlias` (display / legacy cache).
- **Resolution:** override (key ignoring case, then alias field; ambiguous alias = error) -> environment key -> `profiles[<this id>]` (legacy machine.id match warns "run migrate") -> config `machineId` only when the id is undetectable -> legacy `machineAlias` cache -> hostname -> none.
- **register** `--profile F --out TMP [--alias A] [--claim KEY] [--allow-duplicate-alias] ...`: writes only this machine's key; claims its own legacy entry; claims an unclaimed legacy entry only with `--claim KEY` (after an interactive choice); alias used by another profile (ignoring case) = exit 4 unless `--allow-duplicate-alias`; no machine id = exit 3; alias defaults to the short hostname; refreshes `machine.hostname`.
- **migrate** `[--profile F] [--dry-run]`: re-keys legacy profiles in place (alias = old key), refuses one id on two profiles or an id-shaped key that disagrees with machine.id, temp -> validate -> backup -> move, commits profile.json only, pushes. Results: migrated / unchanged / dry-run / refused / failed.
- **exception-set** writes only with owner this (via id, environment or override); **exception-get** ignores another machine's decisions (warning).
- **Exit codes:** 0 ok, 1 not found, 2 error, 3 refused (no machine profile for this machine / no machine id / snapshot), 4 refused (alias in use).
- **Parity:** `~` in config.json `userRepoPath` / `reposPath` expands to HOME; a UTF-16 config.json or profile.json is rejected by both resolvers with one message. PowerShell `Get-JNode` matches path segments case-sensitively, like perl.
- **Logging (design j):** checked temp-file removal and backups (returned as warnings), rejected-push advice (`git pull --rebase`), `GIT_SSH_COMMAND` BatchMode, OS / version guards exit 1.

### Verification (macOS, isolated HOME, injected ids)

- test-profile.sh 689/0 under bash 5 and /bin/bash 3.2; bash and pwsh output and written bytes identical. test-lib.sh 40/0.
- **test-config.sh 85/4 -- CI red.** Its legacy fixture now gets the design's "run migrate" warning, which setup-user-config turns into WARN rows. Owner: batch 4 (setup-user-config / test-config.sh). Not changed here.
- check-pre-commit: known FAILs 6, 20. check-pre-push: known FAIL 7.

| Item | Contract (changes in bold) |
|---|---|
| Arguments | `--repos-path`/`-ReposPath`, `--repo-path`/`-RepoPath` (absolute), `--drives-json`/`-DrivesJson`, `--github-user`/`-GithubUser`, `--user-repo-path`/`-UserRepoPath` (absolute), **`--machine-id ID`/`-MachineId ID`** (this machine's profile key; any case), `--dry-run`/`-DryRun`, `--force`/`-Force` (no effect), `--help`/`-Help`. No arguments: re-derive from profile.json. |

Derived fields are kept when the derivation does not yield them, except a cached `machineId` that the match does not confirm (matched a 2.1 profile or another machine's): removed. Other keys preserved in place. |
| Summary rows | OK created / updated / verified / dry-run; WARN `rebuilt (was invalid)`; an OK row becomes WARN only after a **real** warning. ERROR rows unchanged. DETAIL `<key> updated` (now incl. `machineId`). |
| ACTION rows | **At most one advisory ACTION, written after the config row** (paired with one counted `log_warn`): `Run: aitools user init (registers this machine)` (profile without machine.id) or `Run: aitools user init (re-keys machine profiles by machine id)` (2.1 alias-keyed profile; replaces the resolver's "run: aitools-profile migrate") or `Run: aitools user init (updates this machine's profile)` (stored hostname differs). Precedence init > re-key > update. Error ACTIONs unchanged. |
| Warning classes | handled (config.json / shared-only state) -> info; **advisory** (no machine.id, run migrate) -> info + the one ACTION; **real** (clone/rename, duplicate alias, hostname guess, undetectable id, ...) -> `log_warn`, counted, row WARN. |
| Exit codes | 0 done (warnings possible), 1 any error. Never 2. Never prompts. |
| Callers | `scripts/aitools` / `aitools.ps1` (user init, batch 5) pass the old flag: still accepted; switch to `--machine-id <key>`. Installers pass neither: no change. |

## Batch 3 / lib contract additions (S3-M3-LibKey)

Commits: e5a9d2e (merge of #80 head `feature/m3-b2-lib-wrappers` = the LibFix contract, no conflicts), 693a7e7 (lib + tests), 13eeb57 (merge of this branch's batch 4 head, no conflicts). Report: `.aitools/channel/handoffs/2026-10-21_10cf255a-c-missions/S3-M3-LibKey/S3-M3-LibKey-report.md`. **This PR now carries #80's commits**: merge #80 first, then #86's diff shrinks.

### Why

Re-key design (h) batch 3; S3-M3-Claude Q1 (hostname in the lib contract); S3-M3-B8b Q2 (dotprofile ACTION); S3-M3-Install open Q1 (relay helpers in the lib, for deploy-harness).

### Contract additions (every #80 name unchanged; no top-level assignment)

| Function | bash (new) | PowerShell (new) |
|---|---|---|
| `resolve_machine_profile` / `Resolve-MachineProfile` | `AITLIB_MACHINE_KEY`, `AITLIB_MACHINE_OWNER`, `AITLIB_MACHINE_LEGACY`, `AITLIB_MACHINE_HOSTNAME` | `$script:AitLibMachineKey`, `AitLibMachineOwner`, `AitLibMachineLegacy`, `AitLibMachineHostname` |
| `relay_child_log TEXT` / `Send-ChildLog LINES` | `AITLIB_CHILD_WARNINGS`, `AITLIB_CHILD_ERRORS` | `$script:AitLibChildWarnings`, `AitLibChildErrors` |
| `run_child_relayed LABEL CMD [ARGS...]` / `Invoke-ChildRelayed LABEL SCRIPT [ARGS] [SUMMARYTOOL]` | `AITLIB_CHILD_RC`, `_WARNINGS`, `_ERRORS` | `$script:AitLibChildRc`, `AitLibChildWarnings`, `AitLibChildErrors` |
| `jsonl_record TS SCRIPT LEVEL MSG` / `Write-JsonlRecord TS SCRIPT LEVEL MSG` | -- | -- |

- `AITLIB_MACHINE_KEY`: the matched profiles key ("" when none). `_OWNER`: `this|other|unregistered|unknown|absent` (default `absent`). `_LEGACY`: `true` when the match is still keyed by alias (default `false`). `_HOSTNAME`: this machine's live short hostname, also on shared-only and no-match runs (answers S3-M3-Claude Q1). `AITLIB_MACHINE_ALIAS` is now the profile's `alias` field (its key for a legacy profile). A resolver without these fields (an older embedded copy) leaves the defaults.
- **Return 0 from `resolve_machine_profile` means a profile matched, not that it is this machine's.** A caller that writes per-machine data checks `AITLIB_MACHINE_OWNER = this` (e.g. a hostname match on another machine's id-keyed profile returns 0 with owner `other`).

### Exit-code mapping (documented in both section headers)

| resolver exit | 0 | 1 | 2 | 3 refused | 4 alias in use |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `resolve_user_repo_path` | 0 | 1 shared-only | 3 | 3 | 3 (unexpected) |
| `resolve_machine_profile` | 0 | 1 shared-only | 3 | 3 | 3 (unexpected) |
| `tool_exception_get` (PS: `$null` for every non-0) | 0 | 1 unavailable | 1 | 1 | 1 (unexpected) |
| `tool_exception_record` | 0 | 1 failed | 1 | 3 refused | 1 (unexpected) |

No wrapper returns 2 (D-TS13). Every 3 and 4 is logged with its code and meaning (`... (resolver exit 3: refused)`, `... (resolver exit 4: alias used by another profile -- not a result of this command)`); neither is read as "nothing found".

### Summary rows

- **New:** a dotprofile resolver error (missing or failing resolver) writes one ACTION per run, `see deploy.log -- the dotprofile could not be resolved` (S3-M3-B8b Q2; deduped like the machine-profile ACTION). Callers (B8a/B8b `setup-user-skills`, `setup-user-cursor`) must not add their own.
- Relay: the child writes its own rows. `Invoke-ChildRelayed` adds `ERROR|<SUMMARYTOOL>|script failed (exception)` only for a throw, when SUMMARYTOOL is given (bash has no in-process throw).

### Relay helpers (moved from the installers' "install log helpers", PR #89)

- Same escaping as #89 (backslash, quote, tab, newline escaped; other control characters dropped), same key order (`ts, host, os, script, run_id, level, msg`); bash and PowerShell write byte-identical records for valid UTF-8 text without U+0085/U+2028/U+2029 (tested with `José ✓`); bytes that are not valid UTF-8 give an unparseable bash record, as the #89 installer code did (review m1). `run_id` = `AITOOLS_RUN_ID`, else one random id per process. A failed JSONL write is a detail line (logging.md section 5).
- Counting: warn lines increment `WARNINGS` / `$script:warnings` and `AITLIB_CHILD_WARNINGS`; the exit footers (`COMPLETED with ...`, `FAILED with ...`) are relayed, not counted. Error lines are counted in `AITLIB_CHILD_ERRORS`; `run_child_relayed` / `Invoke-ChildRelayed` return 1 after one `log_error` when the child exited non-zero **or logged an error and exited 0** (stricter than the installer's `run_config_writer`).
- `run_child_relayed` streams (process substitution + an exit-code file, bash 3.2 safe), so a child's log lines show before any prompt it reads from /dev/tty; stdin and stderr stay the caller's.
- **Installers not switched (follow-up, not this batch).** Call-site change for the installer owner:
  - bash `aitools-install.sh`: delete the `jsonl_write` / `relay_child_log` / `run_config_writer` part of the "install log helpers" block (keep `absolute_repos_path`); in the `log` override call `jsonl_record "$ts" "$SCRIPT_NAME" "$level" "$1"`; drop `LOG_JSONL` / `RUN_ID` / `HOST_NAME` / `OS_NAME`; Step 5 becomes `step5_rc=0; run_child_relayed "Step 5: setup-user-config.sh" "${BASH:-bash}" "$CONFIG_WRITER" "${writer_args[@]}" || step5_rc=$?` (the helper has already logged the one error; `step5_rc` keeps `set -e` from exiting). Note `relay_child_log` and `Write-JsonlRecord` have the installers' names and signatures, so the installers' copies shadow the lib's until they are deleted.
  - PowerShell `aitools-install.ps1`: delete `Write-JsonlRecord` / `Send-ChildLogLine` / `Invoke-ConfigWriter` (keep `Get-AbsoluteReposPath`) and `$logJsonl` / `$runId` / `$hostName` / `$osName`; the `Log` override keeps calling `Write-JsonlRecord`; Step 5 becomes `$null = Invoke-ChildRelayed "Step 5: setup-user-config.ps1" $configWriter $writerArgs "aitools config"`.
  - tests/profile/test-config.sh extracts the installer block; after the switch it should exercise the lib functions instead. deploy-harness uses `run_child_relayed` / `Invoke-ChildRelayed` per child.

### Verification (tested: macOS, isolated HOME)

- `bash -n` (bash 5, /bin/bash 3.2), pwsh parser: clean; no top-level `AITLIB_` / `$script:AitLib` assignment.

## Batch 5 -- user init on id-keyed profiles (S3-M3-UserInit)

Commit bb43536. Files: `scripts/aitools`, `scripts/aitools.ps1`, `scripts/profile-check.js`, new `tests/profile/test-user-init.sh` + `tests/profile/user-init-harness.ps1`, `.github/workflows/check.yml` (two steps run the new test).

- **Found first:** both entry points still called the pre-LibFix helper names (`_aitools_profile_run`, `Invoke-AitoolsProfile`, ...), so user init did not run on this branch. And the lib runner (`_aitlib_profile_run` / `Invoke-AitLibProfile`) rewrites an exit 0 without a `via` line to 2; `register` and `migrate` print no `via` line, so every register through the lib reads as an error. User init therefore has its own small runner (INCIDENT for the lib owner).
- **Decision 1:** `--alias <new>` without a terminal creates this machine's profile (register, not a resolve override: Review3 M1). An alias used by another machine's profile (exit 4) is refused without a terminal (ERROR, exit 1). On a terminal it asks y/N, then passes `--allow-duplicate-alias`. The other machine's profile is never rewritten. An alias that names a profile no machine owns claims it.
- **Menu:** profiles no machine owns are numbered and picking one passes `--claim KEY` (Resolver Q2). Another machine's are listed `[-] <alias> (registered to another machine)`, also when the hostname suggests them (Review3 M2), and cannot be picked. Resolver warnings and errors are logged at level; the ones user init resolves itself are logged at info.
- **Decision 2 (pending):** one line per entry point, `AITOOLS_REGISTER_CACHED_ALIAS_SILENTLY=0` / `$script:RegisterCachedAliasSilently = $false`. via=alias asks on a terminal and is ERROR without one; at 1 / `$true` it claims silently. Both values are tested.
- **migrate** runs before resolve (backup, commit, push), so the writer's "re-keys machine profiles" ACTION clears: the ConfigKey merge condition is met.
- **Decision 6:** a missing profile.json is scaffolded (`register --create`: identity from gh/git, this machine under its id), committed and pushed. Path 3 commits, then creates and pushes the private GitHub repo (asked on a terminal, default yes), so the scaffold reaches the remote. No `meta.intent` is written (TODO(PR #96)).
- **Decision 3:** user init has its own run summary, created before the user block and shown before it returns. Every path writes a row, ACTIONs are deduped, and one outcome gets one exit code: not registered at the end = 1, abort in the hooks menu = 2.
- **Writer:** gets `--machine-id <key>` / `-MachineId`, only when the profile is this machine's (ConfigKey Q3).
- **Review3 #82 minors:**
  - (3) see decision 3.
  - (4) A rejected push gets "git pull --rebase, then git push". Without a terminal git cannot prompt (`GIT_TERMINAL_PROMPT=0`, `GIT_SSH_COMMAND` with BatchMode).
  - (5) A profile.json with uncommitted edits is never committed: registration is refused (ERROR), or not refreshed (WARN) when the machine is already registered.
  - (6) `machine.shell`: pwsh on Windows from both entry points, `$SHELL` on macOS/Linux.
  - (7) The dead PS `--alias` scan is removed.
  - (8) The Path 2 clone and `git config` results are checked.
  - The CLAUDE.md scaffold commit gains the identity fallback and loses `|| true`.
  - Path 3 prompts never block.
- **v1 -> v2 migration:** it converts the identity and registers this machine through `register` (id key). It commits profile.json only and no longer writes config.json (decision 7).
- **build-deploy (B5a Q1 / #95):** both entry points resolve the dotprofile (`resolve_user_repo_path` / `Resolve-UserRepoPath`), pull it, and pass it as `AITOOLS_USER_REPO_PATH` (forward slashes), scoped to the build call. The "entry point exports" comments in build-deploy.sh / aitools-profile.sh are now true.
- **profile-check.js:** reports config.json `machineId` and checks it against profile keys (a legacy alias-keyed profile is found by `machine.id`); `machineAlias` is kept.

**Verification (macOS; isolated HOMEs, injected ids, local bare remotes, gh stub):**
- test-user-init: 150/0 under bash 5 + pwsh and 150/0 under /bin/bash 3.2 + pwsh. Every case checks bash and PowerShell exit codes, summary rows, profile.json bytes and remote commits; the pty cases compare the menus.
- test-profile 689/0 (3.2 498/0), test-config 190/0, test-lib 209/0, logging 24/0 + 15/0, managed-files 42/0 + 24/0.
- Syntax: `bash -n` (5, 3.2), the pwsh parser, `node --check`.
- check-pre-commit: step 6 FAIL (known). check-pre-push (isolated HOME): 0 FAIL.

**Not run:** Windows / Git Bash (the runner's pwsh branch, cygpath), real gh, `aitools` sync end to end.

**Batch 5 follow-ups:**
- f473e55 is a test-only fix: CI's default branch is master, so the test's remotes are now created on `main`.
- 26be34e fixes the review findings (https://github.com/nobul-tech/aitools/pull/86#pullrequestreview-5622357229):
  - M1: a relative `reposPath` is refused.
  - M2: on via=config, user init no longer passes `--machine-id`, so the cached `machineId` stays.
  - m1: child scripts run with `| Out-Host` in aitools.ps1, so their output cannot become the exit code.

######## PR #87 (2026-10-23T01:30:14Z) S3-H-Cursor: fan harness rules out to Cursor user rule files (~/.cursor/rules, .md -> .mdc)
https://github.com/nobul-tech/aitools/pull/87  +1532/-6 files=10
FILES: .aitools/channel/handoffs/2026-10-21_10cf255a-c-missions/S3-H-Cursor/S3-H-Cursor-Review-reverify.md, .aitools/channel/handoffs/2026-10-21_10cf255a-c-missions/S3-H-Cursor/S3-H-Cursor-Review-verdict.md, .aitools/channel/handoffs/2026-10-21_10cf255a-c-missions/S3-H-Cursor/S3-H-Cursor-drafts/reference/tool-ops-cursor.md, .aitools/channel/handoffs/2026-10-21_10cf255a-c-missions/S3-H-Cursor/S3-H-Cursor-drafts/registries/tool-ops.json.cursor-entry.json, .aitools/channel/handoffs/2026-10-21_10cf255a-c-missions/S3-H-Cursor/S3-H-Cursor-handback.md, .aitools/channel/handoffs/2026-10-21_10cf255a-c-missions/S3-H-Cursor/S3-H-Cursor-research.md, scripts/setup-user-cursor.ps1, scripts/setup-user-cursor.sh, tests/rules-fanout-cursor/convert-rule.ps1, tests/rules-fanout-cursor/test-rules-fanout-cursor.sh

## Summary

`setup-user-cursor.sh/.ps1` now fan out every `~/.aitools/rules/*.md` into Cursor's user rule files, `~/.cursor/rules/<name>.mdc` (Windows: `%USERPROFILE%\.cursor\rules\`).

- Each rule is converted to the `.mdc` form Cursor actually loads.
- Path-scoped rules get globs anchored the way Cursor resolves them.
- The PR adds a 79-case test suite, the research behind the location, and the protected-file drafts for `/tool-ops`.

## Why

This is the S3-Harness rules fan-out (mission packet section 6). The location is established in `S3-H-Cursor-research.md`:

- **Docs**: https://cursor.com/help/customization/rules.md: "**User rule files** in `~/.cursor/rules` (Windows: `%USERPROFILE%\.cursor\rules`) stay on the machine and do not sync." https://cursor.com/docs/rules.md: "Project rules must use the `.mdc` extension. A plain `.md` file in `.cursor/rules` is ignored".
- **Code**: Cursor IDE 3.13.10 and CLI 2026.05.21 (installed bundles, read-only) share `LocalCursorRulesService.loadRulesFromDirAndAncestors`.
  - It loads `<dir>/.cursor/rules/**/*.mdc` for the workspace and every ancestor, so user rule files reach every workspace under the home directory.
  - A rule needs a leading `---` frontmatter block.
  - The IDE matches a relative glob against the file path relative to the directory that owns `.cursor/rules`, which for a user rule is `$HOME` (`rL`/`aL`; found in review, research section B6).

## Changes

- `scripts/setup-user-cursor.sh` / `.ps1`, new step 4. It sits inside the cursor body markers, so build-deploy's extraction already carries it.
  - **Sources**: `~/.aitools/rules/*.md` only. `*.bak.*` files are skipped. Other files and subdirectories get an info log.
  - **Conversion**:
    - A rule without frontmatter gets `alwaysApply: true`.
    - A Claude Code `paths:` key, in any form (indented or column-0 list, single value, `[a, b]` flow, brace patterns), becomes an indented `globs:` block list with `alwaysApply: false`.
    - Each relative pattern is anchored as `**/<pattern>`. Absolute and `**/` patterns are kept.
    - Other keys and the body are kept. CRLF becomes LF, and a BOM is dropped.
  - **Deploy**: each rule goes through `deploy_managed_file` / `Deploy-ManagedFile` with `ADOPT_PATH ""`, because the harness copy is not the repo source and the target is a converted form. Every return value is handled at the call site. Abort is exit 2 (lib).
  - **Additive**: unmanaged files in `~/.cursor/rules` are preserved and logged. Lib backups are not counted.
  - **Duplicates**: targets are compared case-insensitively. The first source wins, and the next gets `log_error` plus an ERROR row.
  - **Dangling symlink or unreadable source dir**: ERROR (same on both platforms).
  - **Missing or empty source**: info log plus `OK | cursor rules | n/a (no harness rules)`.
  - **Dry run with failures**: ERROR row.
  - **Validation**: each deployed rule must be non-empty, start with `---`, and parse the way Cursor parses it (whole-file `---` split). A misparse is a WARN.
- `tests/rules-fanout-cursor/test-rules-fanout-cursor.sh` (+ `convert-rule.ps1`):
  - Converter golden cases, plus PS1 byte parity (the function is loaded from the script's AST).
  - A resolution model of Cursor's `aL` matcher, including the un-anchored negative. Every repo `paths:` glob is checked to come out anchored.
  - Missing and empty source, first run, rerun, edited target (non-interactive overwrite with a backup), `--force`.
  - Backups and non-`.md` files skipped, case duplicates, dangling symlink, unreadable dir, `---` inside a value, dry run, dry run with a failure.
- Research, drafts, the reviewer's verdict and the hand-back are under `.aitools/channel/handoffs/2026-10-21_10cf255a-c-missions/S3-H-Cursor/`.

## Contract change

The S3-Harness correction (2026-10-22) changes "consumers copy every regular file in `~/.aitools/rules`" to "copy only `*.md`; skip `*.bak.*`".
- Reason: `backup_file` (`scripts/aitools-lib.sh`) writes `<file>.bak.<ts>` next to each harness file it updates, so after a deploy-harness update `~/.aitools/rules` contains backups (S3-H-Core finding, PR #88).
- Effect here: a source `.mdc` or `.txt` in `~/.aitools/rules` is no longer fanned out.
- Separately, for S3-Harness: path-scope translation (Claude Code `paths:` is project-relative; Cursor user-rule globs are `$HOME`-relative) is not in the contract. It is handled here by `**/` anchoring. See the INCIDENT marker in the hand-back.

## Review

The independent review by S3-H-Cursor-Review is at https://github.com/nobul-tech/aitools/pull/87#pullrequestreview-5620875626. It requested changes with 1 blocking finding (B1: un-anchored globs never auto-attach) and 8 non-blocking ones (N1-N8). All nine are fixed in `1a4e31e`, and the contract change is in `118a79c`.

The re-review reported no blocking findings (https://github.com/nobul-tech/aitools/pull/87#pullrequestreview-5621000182). Its three non-blocking items are fixed in `c55005d`:
- R1: PS1 now detects a dangling symlink via `ResolveLinkTarget($true).Exists`.
- R2: comment lines inside a `paths:` list are skipped.
- R3: bash skips the parse check when a file has no frontmatter.

## Verification

- `bash -n scripts/setup-user-cursor.sh`: OK. pwsh parser on `setup-user-cursor.ps1`: OK. No non-ASCII characters in the PS1 code.
- `bash tests/rules-fanout-cursor/test-rules-fanout-cursor.sh`: **78 passed, 0 failed, 1 skipped**. The skip is dupcase, which needs a case-sensitive file system and macOS APFS is case-insensitive. Run on macOS with pwsh 7.
- Isolated-HOME run over the repo's 26 `.claude/rules`: `OK|cursor rules|26 added`. The output was parsed with Cursor's own `cr`/`Sc`/`ur` logic, copied verbatim from the CLI bundle by the reviewer: 9 `fileGlobbed` with `**/` globs, 17 `global`.
- `scripts/check-pre-commit.sh`: only known or expected findings.
  - FAIL step 6: the pre-existing `harvesting/...build-deploy-prefix.sh` 100644.
  - WARN steps 3 and 13: build-deploy not run (S3-H-MDM).
  - WARN step 9: no release notes entry.
- `scripts/check-pre-push.sh`: only the known step 7 FAIL (stale dotprofile `deploy/`).

## Not run

- **Live Cursor canary.** `agent -p` returns "Authentication required. Please run 'agent login' first". Under an isolated HOME, `agent status` says "Not logged in". The real `~/.cursor` was not modified. A 1-minute manual canary is in the research file, section C3.

## Follow-ups

- `reference/cursor-practices.md` says Cursor user rules are "Not file-accessible". That holds for account User Rules, but the file location exists. Needs an update.
- The `~/.cursor/AGENTS.md` relay mirror appears unread by Cursor's loader, which reads `<ancestor>/AGENTS.md`. Proposed as an incident.

- O1: aitools project rules ("(this repo)") become user-level. 17 of them are `alwaysApply: true` in every Cursor chat under `$HOME`. This comes from the contract.
- O2: a removed harness rule's `.mdc` stays behind as preserved.

######## PR #89 (2026-10-23T00:37:33Z) Installer Step 5: relay writer logs and warnings, fix relative repos path and dry-run writes (#79 fix-forward)
https://github.com/nobul-tech/aitools/pull/89  +611/-36 files=7
FILES: .aitools/channel/handoffs/2026-10-21_10cf255a-c-missions/S3-M3-Install-Review/S3-M3-Install-Review-verdict.md, .aitools/channel/handoffs/2026-10-21_10cf255a-c-missions/S3-M3-Install/S3-M3-Install-report.md, scripts/aitools-install.ps1, scripts/aitools-install.sh, scripts/setup-user-config.ps1, scripts/setup-user-config.sh, tests/profile/test-config.sh

## Summary

This is a fix-forward for **#79**. #79 was merged at cc597ec (2026-10-22T20:46:29Z) before its Review3 fixes were pushed to its branch, so they ship here from main.

Commits:
- **97deb5a** fixes Review3's five #79 minors and the logging-audit findings (commander decision 3).
- **2f189a0** fixes the independent review of that commit (S3-M3-Install-Review: 1 major, 2 minors, 1 nit).
- **0c16ec2** adds the mission report and the review verdict.

## Why

- **Review3 #79 m1-m5:** `S3-Profile-Review3-pr79-80-82.md`, section "PR #79".
- **Commander decision 3:** a logging audit of installer Step 5 and setup-user-config.
- **S3-M3-Install-Review:** https://github.com/nobul-tech/aitools/pull/79#pullrequestreview-5620818197

## Changes

### Installers (`aitools-install.sh` / `.ps1`): the "install log helpers" block

- **`jsonl_write` / `Write-JsonlRecord`:** every deploy.jsonl record is one valid JSON line.
  - bash escapes backslash, quote, tab and newline, and drops other control characters. Before, a message with a quote produced an invalid line.
  - PowerShell writes keys in the same order as bash.
- **`run_config_writer` / `Invoke-ConfigWriter` + `relay_child_log` / `Send-ChildLogLine` (Review3 m1 + m2):**
  - Step 5 captures the writer's console lines (bash: stdout; PowerShell: `6>&1`, still in-process) and prints them.
  - It mirrors each structured line to deploy.jsonl, keeping the writer's ts, script and level.
  - It counts the writer's warnings, skipping the writer's own `COMPLETED with N warning(s)` footer (review M1).
  - A failing writer is still one installer error.
- **`absolute_repos_path` / `Get-AbsoluteReposPath` (Review3 m3, review m2):**
  - Step 3 makes a relative repos path absolute, relative to the current directory: that is where the old installer created it.
  - The result is normalised: `../`, and a run from `/`.
  - The conversion is logged.
- **Validator comment (Review3 m4):** `validate_json_config` / `ValidateJsonConfig` stay in the installers. In bash the comment now sits directly above the function.

### Writer (`setup-user-config.sh` / `.ps1`)

- **Dry run (Review3 m5):** it merges into a system temp file and does not create `~/.aitools`. No config, temp file or backup is written. Only the log, `~/.aitools/logs/deploy.log` under the default `AITOOLS_LOG_DIR`, is written.
- **Logging-audit fixes:**
  - The `mktemp -d` result is checked: ERROR `temp dir failed`.
  - Creating `~/.aitools` is checked: ERROR `write failed`.
  - The double-slash check is one perl call with its exit code checked. Before, a two-stage pipe could die of SIGPIPE under pipefail, a silent `set -e` exit.

### Tests (`tests/profile/test-config.sh`)

- **C8b:** a dry run with `~/.aitools` read-only.
- **Installer-helper cases, run in both shells.** The helper block is extracted from each installer and run against the real writer:
  - Step 3: relative, `../`, and from `/`.
  - Step 5: config.json is written; deploy.jsonl gets the writer's lines and they are valid JSON; the warning count equals the writer's own N; the lines are shown on the console.
  - A failing writer is one error, and its ERROR row is kept.
  - A JSONL record with control characters is valid JSON.

## Interface: setup-user-config.sh / .ps1

| Item | Contract |
|---|---|
| Arguments (bash / PowerShell) | `--repos-path`/`-ReposPath`, `--repo-path`/`-RepoPath` (absolute only), `--drives-json`/`-DrivesJson` (JSON array), `--github-user`/`-GithubUser`, `--user-repo-path`/`-UserRepoPath` (absolute), `--machine-alias`/`-MachineAlias`, `--dry-run`/`-DryRun`, `--force`/`-Force` (accepted, no effect), `--help`/`-Help`. With no arguments it re-derives the values from profile.json. |
| Env vars | `HOME`; `AITOOLS_DRY_RUN=1`; `AITOOLS_LOG_DIR`; `AITOOLS_SUMMARY_FILE`; `AITOOLS_ENVIRONMENT` (lib). Through the resolver: `AITOOLS_USER_REPO_PATH` (used for this run only, not saved), `AITOOLS_MACHINE_ALIAS`, `AITOOLS_PROFILE_FILE`. Tests only: `AITOOLS_PROFILE_TEST_MACHINE_ID`, `AITOOLS_PROFILE_TEST_HOSTNAME`, `AITOOLS_PROFILE_TEST_ANY_OS`. |
| Files read | `~/.aitools/config.json`; the dotprofile `profile.json`, through `aitools-profile.* derive-config`; `aitools-json.pl` (bash) or `aitools-json.ps1` (PowerShell). |
| Files written | `~/.aitools/config.json`: temp file beside it -> validate -> `config.json.bak.<ts>` -> move. A dry run writes no config, temp file or backup, and does not create `~/.aitools`. |
| Summary rows (`aitools config`) | **OK:** created, updated, verified, `dry-run: would update`. **WARN:** `rebuilt (was invalid)`; an OK row also becomes WARN when the run warned. **ERROR:** bad arguments, `perl not found` (bash only), resolver missing, `temp dir failed` (new), write failed, derivation failed, merge failed, validation failed, missing bootstrap values. **DETAIL:** `<key> updated`. |
| Other rows | ERROR `profile.json` `invalid (see log)`; ERROR `machine profile` `no machine match`; ACTION rows. |
| Log sinks | `deploy.log`, plus console lines (bash stdout, PowerShell Write-Host). Not `deploy.jsonl`: a caller relays the lines, as the installers do. |
| Exit codes | `0`: done (warnings possible). `1`: any error. Help: 0. Never 2. |
| Interaction | Never prompts; safe for a script to call (deploy-harness). |
| Changes vs cc597ec (the #79 merge) | The dry-run temp file location (and the dry run no longer creates `~/.aitools`). `temp dir failed` is a new ERROR row. `write failed` now also covers "could not create `~/.aitools`". |

  - Step 20 failed there on the dead `tool-support-decision-tree.md` reference; #78 fixed it on main.

######## PR #90 (2026-10-23T01:28:19Z) M3 B8c: check-lib and ait-harvest use the one dotprofile answer
https://github.com/nobul-tech/aitools/pull/90  +264/-15 files=7
FILES: .aitools/channel/handoffs/2026-10-21_10cf255a-c-missions/S3-M3-B8c/S3-M3-B8c-Review-pr90.md, .aitools/channel/handoffs/2026-10-21_10cf255a-c-missions/S3-M3-B8c/S3-M3-B8c-report.md, scripts/ait-harvest.py, scripts/check-lib.ps1, scripts/check-lib.sh, scripts/check-post-push.ps1, scripts/check-post-push.sh

## Summary

M3 batch B8c: the check scripts and the session harvester get their dotprofile the same way as everything else.

- `scripts/check-lib.sh` `resolve_config` and `scripts/check-lib.ps1` `ResolveConfig` now call the lib resolver (`resolve_user_repo_path` / `Resolve-UserRepoPath`). They no longer read `userRepoPath` from config.json themselves.
- `scripts/ait-harvest.py` reads config.json BOM-safely (`utf-8-sig`), so a config.json with a BOM no longer turns off session archiving.

**Stacked on #80** (`feature/m3-b2-lib-wrappers`, merged with origin/main). This PR's own commit is the last one, 4b345cc.

## Why

- **M3 B8c** (S5 plan B8): "check-lib.sh/.ps1 (`resolve_config`), `scripts/ait-harvest.py` (`read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig")` so a BOM config does not silently disable archiving)".
- **SD-7**: there is one answer to "which dotprofile".
- **Commander decision 3**: audit the logging on every path this PR touches.

## Changes

- **check-lib.sh / check-lib.ps1:**
  - The function follows the #80 contract:
    ```bash
    resolve_user_repo_path || rc=$?
    USER_REPO_PATH="$AITLIB_USER_REPO_PATH"
    ```
    In PowerShell: `$rc = Resolve-UserRepoPath; $script:UserRepoPath = $script:AitLibUserRepoPath`. The check-lib names `USER_REPO_PATH` / `$script:UserRepoPath` are unchanged (Review3 #80 M1).
  - `CONFIG_FILE` / `$script:ConfigFile` are still set, because check-pre-push step 7 copies that file.
  - **rc 1 (no dotprofile, shared-only mode):** only a detail line, and no error in a check. Callers keep skipping their dotprofile steps.
  - **rc 3:** the lib already logs the error to the console and deploy.log, and adds an ERROR row when a run summary exists. check-lib adds one info line: "dotprofile unresolved (resolver error above) -- dotprofile steps skip as 'not configured'".
- **Which log gets the resolver's lines:** `resolve_config` runs after `init-logging` and before `check_log_init`, so the lines go to `deploy.log` under the check's init-logging name (for example `check-pre-push`). They do not go to checks.log. `check_log_init` then resets `ERRORS` / `WARNINGS`, but checks report through PASS/FAIL counts, so this has no effect on how a check passes or fails. This is the bridge pattern in reference/script-standards-detail.md.
- **Behaviour change in checks:**
  - `AITOOLS_USER_REPO_PATH` is now honoured.
  - If config.json has no usable `userRepoPath`, the dotprofile is found by convention (`<reposPath>/aitools-<githubUser>`) or by scan.
  - A config `userRepoPath` that points to a missing directory now falls through to convention. Before, the check kept the dead path and skipped.
  - The default case is unchanged: on this Mac, pre-commit step 11 resolves `/Users/new-jose/repos/aitools-nobul-jose` (via config).
- **ait-harvest.py `read_config_user_repo` (logging audit):**
  - It reads with `utf-8-sig`.
  - It catches `ValueError`, which covers `JSONDecodeError` and `UnicodeDecodeError`. Before, a UTF-16 config crashed the helper with an uncaught exception.
  - A non-object config now gets a warning; before, it crashed with an `AttributeError`.
  - A non-string `userRepoPath` now gets a warning; before, it crashed with a `TypeError`.
  - A configured dotprofile that does not exist is now a warning, not a detail line. Archiving stops in that case, so it should not be quiet.

## Verification

All runs on macOS with isolated HOMEs (mktemp / tempfile).
- **Syntax:** `bash -n` (bash 5 and /bin/bash 3.2) on check-lib.sh and the four check scripts that source it, the pwsh parser on check-lib.ps1, check-pre-push.ps1 and check-pre-commit.ps1, and `python3 -m py_compile` on ait-harvest.py: all clean.
- **resolve_config / ResolveConfig cases** (scratch `test-checklib.sh`, a driver that copies the check-script block order; the PS driver runs with `$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"`). Cases: config, BOM config, none (shared-only, no `[error]` in deploy.log), `AITOOLS_USER_REPO_PATH`, missing env path (console `[error]` + info line), stale config path -> convention.
  - bash + pwsh: **12/0**
  - /bin/bash 3.2 + pwsh: **12/0**
  - Negative control against the pre-change scripts: **0/12**
  - PS uses `AITOOLS_PROFILE_TEST_ANY_OS=1`, as test-lib.sh does.
- **read_config_user_repo cases** (scratch `test_harvest_bom.py`): plain, UTF-8 BOM, UTF-16, list, int path, missing dir, no key, corrupt.
  - **8/8**
  - Negative control against the old file: **3/8** (BOM was rejected; UTF-16, list and int path crashed; missing dir was detail only).
- **Suites:**
  - test-profile 319/0;
  - test-config 89/0;
  - test-lib 108/0 (/bin/bash 3.2 `--bash-only` 44/0);
  - tests/logging: sh 24/0, ps 15/0 (5 skipped).
- **check-pre-commit:** step 6 FAIL is known. WARNs 3, 5, 9 and 11 (11 is the real dotprofile, found through the resolver).
- **check-pre-push:** step 7 FAIL is known (dotprofile deploy/ is stale). WARNs 1, 5 and 6. Step 3 passes.

## Not run

- **Windows:**
  - check-*.ps1 are Windows-only (OS guard);
  - aitools-profile.ps1 on a real Windows machine;
  - Git Bash path forms. On Git Bash, `USER_REPO_PATH` is now in `/c/...` form (from `cygpath -u`), not the raw config value. Every consumer passes it to `git -C` / `[ -d ]`, which accept that form.
- The SessionEnd and SessionStart hooks run end to end with a BOM config.
- check-post-push.sh and check-prereq-detection.sh were syntax-checked only.

## Review fixes (9779372)

Independent review: https://github.com/nobul-tech/aitools/pull/90#pullrequestreview-5621229237. It found 0 blockers, 1 major, 4 minors and 3 nits.

- **M1, fixed.** check-post-push step 5 could PASS while archiving was off. Its dotprofile could now come from env, convention or scan, but ait-harvest archives only to config.json `userRepoPath`.
  - check-lib now also sets `USER_REPO_VIA` / `$script:UserRepoVia`.
  - Step 5 in check-post-push.sh/.ps1 warns whenever the via is not `config`.
  - **Scope extension:** check-post-push.* were not in the B8c file list. I changed them because this PR caused the regression, and no parallel mission owns them.
  - **Verified:** the reviewer's repro now gives WARN (it gave PASS); the real check-post-push still gives PASS through config.
- **m1, fixed.** The comments now cite the resolver functions instead of a user-repo.md section that does not exist.
- **n1, fixed.** A `userRepoPath` of `false` or `0` now gets the non-string warning.
- **Not changed:**
  - m2: the callers' skip text on rc 3 (follow-up);
  - m3: promote the scratch tests (follow-up);
  - m4: resolver `~` and UTF-16 parity, which belongs to #80/#86;
  - n3: step 7 says "see checks.log" (pre-existing, step 7).

## Protected-file drafts awaiting approval

None.

## Follow-ups

- **H5 (B5 owner, scripts/build-deploy.sh):** once build-deploy uses the resolver, check-pre-push step 7 has to pass `AITOOLS_USER_REPO_PATH=<its temp dotprofile copy>` and unset `AITOOLS_PROFILE_FILE` / `AITOOLS_MACHINE_ALIAS`. Otherwise an exported value steers the rebuild into the real dotprofile. Today build-deploy reads config.json directly, so step 7 is safe at this head. This PR does not change step 7.
- **ait-harvest.py and the resolver:** ait-harvest still reads only config.json, with no env, convention or scan. Using the resolver from a Python hook helper would mean calling across languages (bash/pwsh from Python), and that needs approval under cross-platform.md. Suggestion: leave it until config.json is generated on every machine (decision 7). Then config.json `userRepoPath` is the resolver's own answer.
- **Promote the scratch tests into tests/:** `tests/harvest/` does not exist. Suggestion: add `tests/profile/test-checklib.sh` and a `tests/harvest/test_config.py` in a later batch, and run them in CI.

######## PR #91 (2026-10-23T01:28:53Z) M3 B8a: settings and skills (sh) use the one dotprofile resolver

## Summary

M3 B8a: `setup-user-settings.sh`, `setup-user-settings.ps1` and `setup-user-skills.sh` find the dotprofile through the lib resolver (`resolve_user_repo_path` / `Resolve-UserRepoPath`) instead of reading `config.json` `userRepoPath` themselves.

**Stacked on #80** (and through it #79, #77, #76), with origin/main merged in. This PR's own commits are e639df2 and c719630.

**Merge gate: merge with or after B5** (see Follow-ups). `aitools` rebuilds deploy/ on every run (`scripts/aitools:1524-1531`). Until B5 lands, the generated settings scripts fail with `resolver missing`.

## Why

- **SD-7:** one resolver answers "which dotprofile". The resolver also honours `AITOOLS_USER_REPO_PATH`, the `<reposPath>/aitools-<githubUser>` convention and the scan, which the direct config read did not.
- **M3 B8a** (S5-Profile plan B8; S3-M3Lead plan).
- **Commander decision 3:** logging audit of the touched paths.

## Changes

Every call follows the #80 contract: call, capture the rc (no `|| true`), copy the result global right after.

```bash
URP_RC=0
resolve_user_repo_path || URP_RC=$?
USER_REPO_PATH="$AITLIB_USER_REPO_PATH"
```
```powershell
$urpRc = Resolve-UserRepoPath
$userRepoPath = $script:AitLibUserRepoPath
```

| rc | setup-user-settings (.sh/.ps1) | setup-user-skills.sh |
|---|---|---|
| 0 found | unchanged: legacy migration + `sync_managed_json` | unchanged: dotprofile skills win, adopt offer |
| 1 shared-only | `log_warn` + WARN `claude settings` `not synced: no dotprofile` | shared skills only (as before) |
| 3 resolver error (lib logged `log_error` + ERROR `dotprofile`) | `log_error` + ERROR `claude settings` `not synced: dotprofile error`; no sync | `log_error` + ERROR rows for `claude skills` and `cursor skills` (`not deployed: dotprofile error`); nothing discovered, deployed or offered for adoption. S3-M3Lead decision: shared copies must never replace deployed dotprofile versions |

- **Summary text.** The old WARN text `no profile (run aitools user init)` was 34 characters, over the 30-character limit (script-standards-detail "Summary detail text rules"). "skipped" is avoided: it is the governed menu outcome.
- **ACTION rows.** None added. The wrapper owns ACTION rows (#80 contract). Before this PR, rc 1 had no ACTION either.
- **Header comments** name the resolver instead of config.json.
- **Extraction markers** (`BEGIN/END settings body`, `skills body`, `skill discovery`, `exit`): intact.

  - The same two, with a resolver error (`AITOOLS_USER_REPO_PATH` names a missing directory). Results: exit 1, both ERROR rows, no settings.json, no dotprofile skill, and no false `deploy failed` row.
  - setup-user-settings.ps1, the same three cases. It ran as a copy with the OS guard stripped, under pwsh 7 on macOS, with `AITOOLS_PROFILE_TEST_ANY_OS=1`.
