######## PR #116 (2026-10-27T06:36:11Z) Installers and user init use the lib relay (A-1, A-12..A-18)
https://github.com/nobul-tech/aitools/pull/116  +908/-197 files=11
FILES: .aitools/channel/handoffs/2026-10-21_10cf255a-c-missions/S3-MF-Relay/S3-MF-Relay-ReviewB-pr116.md, .aitools/channel/handoffs/2026-10-21_10cf255a-c-missions/S3-MF-Relay/S3-MF-Relay-report.md, .github/workflows/check.yml, scripts/aitools, scripts/aitools-install.ps1, scripts/aitools-install.sh, scripts/aitools-lib.ps1, scripts/aitools-lib.sh, scripts/aitools.ps1, tests/profile/test-install.sh, tests/profile/test-user-init.sh

## Summary

FIX-2 of M3-FINISH item 7. The installers' Step 5 and `aitools user init` now run their child scripts through the lib relay (`run_child_relayed` / `Invoke-ChildRelayed`), and the installers' private relay copies are gone. As a result:

- child warnings and errors are counted;
- every failed child gets an ERROR summary row;
- the children's lines reach deploy.jsonl.

An unwritable deploy.jsonl is one warning, and the run goes on. Step 3 failures get an ERROR row and skip Step 5. A missing `--alias` value is logged through the lib.

Audit findings: A-1, A-12, A-13, A-14, A-15, A-16, A-17, A-18.

**Branch:** `fix/mf-installer-relay` is stacked on #115 (`fix/mf-lib-relay`): PR A's head 4c55ec0, then a `--no-ff` merge of its review fixes 2ae798a. FIX-2's own commits are 1e44578, 7a16400 and ccccbd8.

## Why

| id | sev | problem (audit, at 8a9dcb5) |
|---|---|---|
| A-1 | medium | The installers defined their own `jsonl_write` / `relay_child_log` / `run_config_writer` (PS: `Write-JsonlRecord` / `Send-ChildLogLine` / `Invoke-ConfigWriter`), which shadowed the lib's copy. The two copies had already diverged: the bash copy did not count error lines, the PS copy did not strip CR/ANSI, and the run_id came from separate code. |
| A-12 | medium | user init ran setup-user-config and setup-user-hooks directly. Their warnings were not counted, and none of their lines reached deploy.jsonl. |
| A-13 | low | A non-zero exit from setup-user-hooks (other than 2 or 127) gave no ERROR row. |
| A-14 | low | user init ran `rm -f "$AITOOLS_SUMMARY_FILE"` unchecked. |
| A-15 | low | `--alias` without a value printed a raw stderr line before the lib loaded. |
| A-16 | medium | The bash installer's deploy.jsonl append was unguarded under `set -e`, so an unwritable file ended the installer mid-step with no summary. |
| A-17 | low | A relative repos path that could not be resolved gave an error with no row, and Step 5 still ran. In PS there was no failure branch at all. |
| A-18 | low | A Step 5 writer that exited non-zero gave one error and no row. |

## Changes

### A-1, A-18: installers (`scripts/aitools-install.sh` / `.ps1`)

- **Removed:** the private relay bodies and the JSONL globals (`LOG_JSONL`, `RUN_ID`, `HOST_NAME`; PS `$logJsonl`, `$runId`, `$hostName`, `$osName`).
- **Log override:** the `log` / `Log` override calls the lib's `jsonl_record` / `Write-JsonlRecord`.
- **Step 5:**
  - bash: `run_child_relayed --summary-tool "aitools config" "Step 5: setup-user-config.sh" ...`
  - PS: `Invoke-ChildRelayed "Step 5: setup-user-config.ps1" $Writer $WriterArgs "aitools config"`
  - A writer that dies before writing a row gets `ERROR|aitools config|script failed (exit N)`. A writer that wrote its own ERROR row gets no second one (#115's rule).
- **Kept as thin wrappers:** the installer block still has `jsonl_write`, `run_config_writer` and `Invoke-ConfigWriter`, because tests/profile/test-config.sh calls those names and another mission owns that file. They have no logic of their own (see Follow-ups). PS `Write-JsonlRecord` is now the lib's.
- **Run id:** both installers export `AITOOLS_RUN_ID` (6 random bytes as hex) when it is unset. The installer's records, its relayed children and any child that relays its own children (deploy-harness, #106) then share one run_id.

### A-16: lib (`scripts/aitools-lib.sh` / `.ps1`)

- The first failed write in `jsonl_record` / `Write-JsonlRecord` is one `log_warn`: `deploy.jsonl: cannot write <path> -- structured log records are skipped for the rest of this run`. Later records are skipped.
- The flag is set before the warning, so the installer's log override, which writes a record for every log line, cannot recurse.
- PS writes the exception text as a detail line, so the warning text matches bash.

### A-17: installer Step 3

- New helpers `resolve_repos_dir` / `Resolve-ReposDir`. A path that cannot be created gets ERROR `aitools config` `repos dir not created`; one that cannot be resolved gets `repos path not absolute`. Each is one error, and Step 5 is skipped with an info line.
- **Bug found by the new test:** in PS, `New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -ErrorAction Stop` returned `$null` with no error when a parent was a file (pwsh 7, macOS; probe below). The old Step 3 carried on silently there. It now uses `[IO.Directory]::CreateDirectory` and then checks with `Test-Path -PathType Container`.

### A-12, A-13: user init (`scripts/aitools` / `aitools.ps1`)

- setup-user-config and setup-user-hooks run through the relay, with summary tools `aitools config` and `claude hooks`. PS runs both in-process.
- **Git Bash exception (review B M-1):** on Git Bash the writer is relayed through `pwsh -File`, but the hooks child runs directly. Its PowerShell review menus write their prompt with `[Console]::Write` and no newline, so the relay's line reader would hide the prompt until the user answered. The direct path keeps the relay's outcome: one error and the same row rule. On that path the hooks child's lines do not reach deploy.jsonl (follow-up).
- The fixed `ERROR|aitools config|setup-user-config failed` row is replaced by the relay rule: the writer's own ERROR row, or else the relay's `script failed (exit N)`.
- Exit 2 from hooks still returns 2 (D-TS13). The relay now records it as one error plus `ERROR|claude hooks|aborted (exit 2)` when the hook script wrote no row.
- A missing hooks script keeps its own `setup script missing` row.

### A-14: user init (bash)

- A stale run summary that cannot be removed is now a `log_warn`.

### A-15: `--alias` without a value

- **bash:** the error is queued while parsing and logged through the lib after `logging_init` (`--alias needs a value (aitools user init --alias <name>)`, exit 1). A value that starts with `-` counts as missing.
- **PS:** a missing value is a pwsh parameter-binding error raised before the script runs. That covers `--alias` as the last word, and `--alias` followed by one of the script's switches such as `--force` (corrected by review B m-1). It cannot be logged through the lib. A value that looks like an option (e.g. `-x`) gets the same error as bash after the lib loads.

### Tests

- **`tests/profile/test-install.sh` (new):** it extracts the installers' "install log helpers" and "install log override" blocks, in bash 5, bash 3.2 and pwsh. Cases:
  - step5-warn: the warning is counted; the lines reach deploy.jsonl with one run_id, and the console.
  - step5-crash: one error and the relay's row.
  - step5-ownrow: no second row.
  - jsonl-blocked: deploy.jsonl is a directory. One warning, and the run reaches the end.
  - Step 3: relative path, uncreatable path, and unresolvable path (bash, skipped as root).
- **`tests/profile/test-user-init.sh`:**
  - hook-abort: the `aborted (exit 2)` row.
  - hook-crash: exactly one relay row.
  - relay-jsonl: the hook warning and the writer lines reach deploy.jsonl in both shells.
  - alias-novalue (bash): `--alias` and `--alias --force`.
- **`.github/workflows/check.yml`:** test-install runs on macOS (bash 5 and 3.2) and Linux.

## Assumptions

- **I-1 (deploy.jsonl definition, undecided; draft #162):** I used the audit's suggested answer: deploy.jsonl mirrors every child setup script run through the lib relay. Applied to installer Step 5 and to user init's two children.
  - The other installer steps (`validate_and_run` / `Invoke-ValidatedScript`: gh, tools, Step 21 deploy scripts) and the `aitools` sync deploy loop still run their children directly. They are not audit findings. Relaying them would also move every setup script's stdout off the terminal (tools' progress output, ordering against `/dev/tty` prompts) and change the installer's error rows. Listed under Follow-ups.
- **Warnings counted in user init:** the relay adds the children's warnings to user init's WARNINGS, as A-12 asks. user init writes no rows after its children and has no exit footer, so the count cannot be seen in the panel today. The tests check the relayed lines (console, deploy.jsonl), not the counter.
- **A user abort in the hooks review** is now an ERROR row (`aborted (exit 2)`) plus one counted error, as #115 decided and its review accepted. Before, it was only an info line.

## Merge order

   - this PR's removal of `RUN_ID` / `LOG_JSONL` / `HOST_NAME` and the new `AITOOLS_RUN_ID` export;
   - #106's Step 8b `run_harness_step` / `Invoke-HarnessStep`, which already use the lib relay. They could also pass `--summary-tool "harness files"`.

######## PR #131 (2026-10-27T09:57:16Z) Back up the dotprofile deploy/ and swap the build in only on success
https://github.com/nobul-tech/aitools/pull/131  +301/-7 files=7
FILES: .claude/rules/deploy-paths.md, .cursor/rules/deploy-paths.mdc, .github/workflows/check.yml, reference/managed-file-deployment.md, reference/user-repo.md, scripts/build-deploy.sh, tests/build-deploy/test-build-deploy-backup.sh

## Summary

`build-deploy.sh` now backs up the dotprofile `deploy/` before it builds and replaces it only when the whole build succeeded (incident #86, MERGE-ALL decision A4).

## Why

Before, the swap was `rm -rf <dotprofile>/deploy` then `mv` from `$TMPDIR`. If the `mv` failed, or crossed file systems and stopped part way, the last good `deploy/` was gone and nothing was backed up. Commander, 2026-10-26: "do a backup of the deploy and then do a rm -rf if necessary", then "Back up, then rebuild": keep 5 backups, build in a temp dir, swap only on success.

## Changes

- `scripts/build-deploy.sh`
  - Before the build: copies `<dotprofile>/deploy/` to `<dotprofile>/deploy.bak.<UTC>/` (`-N` suffix on a same-second clash) and checks the copy with `diff -rq`. Then prunes to the newest 5 and logs each prune. If the backup cannot be made, the build stops and `deploy/` is left as it was. This is stricter than config-file-safety.md's "non-fatal" on purpose: `deploy/` is never replaced without a backup.
  - Warns when the dotprofile's git does not ignore the backup, and names the fix. `aitools user init` already writes `*.bak.*`, which covers it.
  - The swap:
    1. The build moves into `deploy.swap.bak.XXXXXX` beside `deploy/`, on the same file system.
    2. The old `deploy/` is renamed aside.
    3. The new tree is renamed in.
    4. Only then is the old tree deleted, and stale generated files go with it.
  - If a swap step fails, the old `deploy/` is put back and the backup is kept.
  - The header documents this.
- `tests/build-deploy/test-build-deploy-backup.sh` (new): isolated HOME, fixture dotprofile git repos, stub `pwsh`. Four cases: success (swap, stale file gone, backup identical, pruned 6 to 5 oldest first, no swap dir, git ignores backups), late failure (PS1 validation; old `deploy/` unchanged, backup kept, git clean), first build (no backup), and a dotprofile without the ignore line (warning).
- `.github/workflows/check.yml`: runs the test on macOS (bash 5 and `/bin/bash` 3.2) and on Linux.
- Docs: `.claude/rules/deploy-paths.md` and `.cursor/rules/deploy-paths.mdc` (the "deletes and rebuilds" sentence), `reference/managed-file-deployment.md` "Backup Policy", and the `reference/user-repo.md` tree.

## Verification (tested: macOS)

- `bash -n scripts/build-deploy.sh`: clean. `check-hook-parity.py`: 18 hooks OK.
- New test: 24 passed, 0 failed under bash 5 and under `/bin/bash` 3.2.
- Negative control: the same test against origin/main's `build-deploy.sh` fails 9 of 24, every backup assertion.
- `check-pre-commit`: only step 6 FAILs (known: a harvesting file's mode).
- `check-pre-push`: only step 7 FAILs (known: the dotprofile `deploy/` is stale until the post-merge rebuild). Step 7's rebuild into the temp copy ran with this build-deploy and succeeded.

## Not run

- Windows / Git Bash. The test does not reach the Windows PS1 validation branch, and CI does not run the test on Windows.
- The real dotprofile rebuild. MERGE-ALL runs it after this merges.

## Protected files written as a version (review after, MERGE-ALL decision A2)

`.claude/rules/deploy-paths.md`, `.cursor/rules/deploy-paths.mdc`, `reference/managed-file-deployment.md`, `reference/user-repo.md`. Each edit is a factual description of the new behaviour. The diff is above.

## Follow-ups

- Incident #86: close it once this has merged and the real rebuild has run (correctiveAction: this PR). That goes through the incident-ledger work, not this PR.
- `shared/skills/delegate/SKILL.md`, `shared/skills/tool-adopt/SKILL.md` and `shared/rules/hook-lifecycle.md` still say build-deploy "deletes and rebuilds" the real dotprofile `deploy/`. The isolated-HOME rule still holds, but the reason is now out of date. Those files belong to other running work (D-DEL5) and are left for the docs scrub.
